ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.

ASSUMPTIONS AND INTERFACES
Every diagram connection is direct; no added bridge reactions or input adapter.
m_in, m_D1, m_D2 and m_c are shared only at the explicitly drawn interfaces.
m_out is the neuron signal output, produced by r_Nw and r_Nb.
No selected role participates in an unrequested selected reaction.
Removal arrows to empty set mean conversion to unlabelled products, not destruction of matter.
consuming_N = true: r_Nw consumes m_c and produces m_out.
m_I and m_b are externally supplied/buffered assumptions; replenishment is not mapped.
Fast/slow names identify topology roles; kinetic separation has not been established.
Connectivity validated: 1. Kinetics validated: 0. Flux feasibility validated: 0.
The net stoichiometric model does not establish enzyme regulation or the required circuit dynamics.
